## STR keyframe continuation — 2026-10-25

- Delta keys preserve blend modes and material preset, as the original processor does. Key transitions preserve the preceding key's age for animation mode 5.
- Modes 1-5 now retain Ref arithmetic, signed results and truncation. The executable confirms that reverse-loop mode reads the quotient left by a preceding layer and initially uses the z argument slot. Preserved this quirk instead of normalizing it to a repeated wrap. ProcessEZ2STR now accepts and retains the original x/y/z arguments for its renderer.
- Validation: five silent C++ suites pass with 178 checks (120 lifecycle/STR), five Python tests pass, release build and approved deployment pass. It now detaches through messages 25/24 and the world deletes it. A production-body regression check covers rejection, replacement and exactly-once reclamation. Super Angel's existing guessed asset/fallback behavior is still an unfinished emitter port.

The Entry2 PP_HEAL emitter uses: four distinct GI ring profiles, four RNG draws, exact ring-blue texture and EF_Portal wave with relative y -150. Parent position follows the master on every call; emission occurs only at frame zero.
- Primitive handlers no longer increment the shared frame counter. `CEffectPrim::OnProcess` dispatches at the current frame, exits on failure, then increments exactly once and checks inclusive duration. Explicit handler counter changes are preserved.
- Validation: release build succeeds, five Python tests pass and all five silent C++ suites pass (162 C++ checks, including 104 lifecycle checks). Approved explicit OldRO deployment succeeds; source/destination SHA-256 both `E03683504DD9E6F6DED3B7E0B8ADC0948047151EFA3F89863D56BC328A8D5FA1`. No media or live session was used. Helper functions that internally call STR and have their own return/side-effect behavior still require individual ports.

## Lifecycle continuation — 2026-10-25

- Replaced the skill effect's wall-clock catch-up loop and primitive/alpha/STR lifetime extensions with one handler dispatch, one counter increment and `stateCnt < duration`. Handler failure returns before the outer increment. Removed synthetic infinite-loop flags on real ids 321 and 601. RSW map emitters retain their existing scheduler separately.
- Primitives now join the world's object list, process/render there, and unregister from their parent when deleted. Parent destruction/message 25 detaches rather than deletes primitives; the world reclaims them. Actor exit/message 24 clears the master instead of allocating a permanent world anchor. Primitive outer lifetime is inclusive (`stateCnt <= duration`), with the reference life-99 gate exception for numeric ids 0 and 0x10.
- Ported `CAbleToMakeEffect::SendMsg` cases 23, 84, 85, 86, 87, 109 and 120. Actor messages now delegate to this base. Begin-cast message 85 preserves Ref's launch/remove/relaunch order, including the first positive-id double initialization; message 86 reuses an existing target effect. Duration zero is passed through. Removal handles id zero and only the first matching attachment.
- Effect message 14 copies both vectors. Removed invented message-109 effect behavior and message-14 Grim Tooth detachment/flight/duration rewriting. Grim Tooth now retains its Init origin, subtracts its two-unit direction step in Ref order, emits every third frame, consumes the two discarded RNG draws, and requests only `effect\stone.bmp`. Its downstream primitive update/render still needs the complete WP5 port.
- Validation: x64 release build passes; five Python tests and five silent C++ suites pass (98 C++ checks, including 40 lifecycle/casting/Grim Tooth checks). ## WP10.6 — scope clarification — 2026-10-27

the plan targets gameplay skill/spell state, effects, and animations; it does not require a 1:1 copy of legacy UI windows or controls. The bounded guild-skill invalidation/row model and `0x01CD` spell-list receiver are retained as groundwork because they do not touch existing Modern Qt UI code. Their missing legacy window factories, rendering, layout, and input flows are not plan blockers. `0x0162` guild-skill packet state remains in scope. `0x0145` image presentation and `0x01CD` auto-spell list presentation are UI-only. Static packet registration count is inventory only and does not define gameplay acceptance. #

## WP5A — `RenderHitLine` texture frame and opacity correction — 2026-10-28

The initial renderer port used the segment frame selector as vertex opacity and supplied full-range UVs. Reinspection of Ref/PE `RenderTeiRect` call arguments showed the actual behavior: skip when `alphaB - segment*5 <= 0`, keep alpha at 255, and map each segment to V coordinates `texturePart/21` through `(texturePart+1)/21` with U reversed across the strip width. Added a dedicated submission helper for that exact topology and strengthened `check_hitline_render.py` to verify UV orientation, per-segment frame selection, and ONE blending. Direct renderer and caller comparisons pass; Release build is pending.

## WP5A — `RenderHitLine` call overload clarification — 2026-10-28

The previous correction misread the overloaded `RenderTeiRect` call. The actual canonical signature at `HighPriest.exe.c:436484` takes `alpha` before the four vertices and has one texture index plus `F1` for absolute coordinates; effect 376's renderer call passes `alphaB - v58`, `TN=0`, `F1=1`, RGB `(255,v32,v33)`, and full UVs `(0,1,0,1)`. Therefore segment fade belongs in output alpha, with the segment skipped when that alpha is nonpositive. Restored the original full-range UV/segment-alpha implementation, removed the unnecessary UV helper, and strengthened the comparator to pin the exact overload call arguments. Direct renderer and caller comparators pass. The initial attempt to build the caller exposed an unmapped `vecBNow` field; the actual structure stores this position in `sparkX/Y/Z`, which is now implemented. Release app build passed after this field correction; parity-test rebuild and CTest remain pending.

## WP10.8 — Ankle Snare trap trigger lifecycle — 2026-10-28

Added `ankle_snare_trap_trigger_lifecycle.tsv` for the source-supported packet sequence: job145 entry, 0x0088 target AID2 fixed to the trap cell (10,20), 0x01AC update for trap AID100, and later 0x0120 expiry removal. Production replay verifies the target cell and runtime actor coordinates, confirms the update leaves the trap at job145, and checks cleanup from the world skill/object lists. Source comparison: `skill.c:7496-7513` condition `UNT_ANKLESNARE && sg->val2 == 0 && tsc`; success applies SC_ANKLE, moves target to trap coordinates, sends fix-position, stores target AID, then sends skill-unit update; failure still updates the unit and uses the 3000 ms fallback. The branch then sets lifetime, disables further interval processing, and clears range. `clif.c:1662` defines 0x0088 `<id>.L <x>.W <y>.W`; `clif.c:4259` defines 0x01AC `<id>.L`. The client routes these to `HandleActorSetPosition` (`src/gamemode/GameModePacket.cpp:7533-7577`) and `HandleSkillUpdate` (`:4820-4827`, message119 for jobs139..153). There is no damage notification or job140 transition; the `SC_ANKLE` status maps to blank icon (`status.c:93,182`), so no status icon packet is expected. Expiry timing is server-owned and represented by explicit removal in the packet fixture. Focused test passes1/1 and full replay CTest passes63/63.

## WP10.8 — Shockwave Trap trigger lifecycle — 2026-10-28

Added `shockwave_trap_trigger_lifecycle.tsv` for job148 Shockwave Trap: visible entry, duplicate entry hides it, 0x01AC reveals it, 0x01D7 transitions it to used-trap job140, and 0x0120 removes it. Production replay confirms the job transitions and world cleanup. Job148 initializes the trap with Ref effect145 and `m_effectId=-1`; the replay checks that effect145 launches exactly once at entry and job140 launches no saved effect. Server source maps HT_SHOCKWAVE skill118 to UNT_SHOCKWAVE/job148 (`skill.h` enums); trigger calls `skill_trap_splash` and changes to UNT_USED_TRAPS with a 1500 ms limit (`skill.c:7523-7538`). Splash applies `status_percent_damage` to SP only, at `skill.c:705-706,9862-9865`; the affected player's parameter update is 0x00B0 (`clif.c:2587`, SP_SP=7), while the observer-side map stream has no 0x01DE damage notification. Client entry behavior at `Ref/Skill.cpp` message45 job148 branch launches effect145, sets no saved effect; job140 message47 therefore launches none. Packet paths 0x01AC and 0x01D7 confirm visibility and used-trap state. Focused CTest passes1/1; freshly built Release replay suite passes64/64.

## Release build repair — 2026-10-28

The OpenMidgard Win32 Release build was failing in `RenderDevice.cpp`: on this target `VkImage` and `VkImageView` alias the same non-dispatchable integer type, so two overload definitions collide and their call sites become ambiguous. Replaced them with one type-generic conversion helper that handles pointer and integer Vulkan handle representations. `cmake --build build --config Release --target open_midgard` now completes and copies `open-midgard.exe` to the local OldRO output directory.
